>> 1974 PERSONAL PROBLEMS CODE

                 THIS NOTE USED IN CONJUNCTION WITH QUESTIONS A9A, A9B,
                 B1A(1), B1A(2), B1A(3), AND B1B


                 100.  NO PROBLEM, E.G., EVERYTHING'S OK

                 QUALITY OF LIFE--ABSTRACTIONS ABOUT LIFE STYLE OR SITUATION
                 -----------------------------------------------------------
                 -----------------------------------------------------------


                   SELF-LEVEL
                   ----------

                 211.  IMMOBILITY, E.G., I JUST CAN'T GET AROUND LIKE I USED
                                   TO; OR, I'M STUCK HERE.

                 212.  DISCRIMINATION, E.G., THEY JUST DON'T CARE ABOUT ME
                                      (NOT "PEOPLE LIKE ME" WHICH IS CODED AT
                                      THE CLASS-LEVEL).

                 213.  TIME PRESSURE, E.G., THERE ISN'T ENOUGH TIME TO GET
                                      THINGS DONE; OR, I CAN'T BE IN TWO
                                      PLACES AT ONCE; OR, EVERYTHING IS SO
                                      RUSHED.

                 214.  RESIGNATION, E.G., I'M JUST TRYING TO GET BY
                                    (NOT FINANCIAL, E.G., "TRYING TO MAKE
                                    ENDS MEET"  WHICH IS CODED UNDER
                                    ECONOMY/WEALTH).

                 215.  EDUCATION, E.G., GETTING GOOD SCHOOLING (OR TRAINING)
                                  TO BETTER MYSELF.

                 216.  FULFILLMENT, E.G., DULL OR UNINTERESTING WORK OR
                                  JOB (NOT FINANCIAL, E.G., "UNDERPAID"
                                  WHICH IS CODED UNDER ECONOMY/WEALTH).

                 217.  LIFE ANXIETY, E.G., THE DECISIONS I HAVE TO MAKE;
                                     THE RESPONSIBILITIES I HAVE; WHAT I AM
                                     GOING TO DO WITH MY LIFE; LOSS OF CLOSE
                                     FRIENDS AND RELATIVES.

                 218.  PERSONAL APPEARANCE, E.G., I DON'T LIKE THE WAY I
                                            LOOK; MY WEIGHT; ETC.

                 219.  GENERAL COMMENTS ABOUT "SELF", OR LIFE SITUATION
                                        NOT SPECIFICALLY CODED IN 211-218,
                                        GENERAL PROBLEMS OF LIFE.


                   FAMILY-LEVEL
                   ------------

                 221.  COHESION, E.G., THIS FAMILY IS FLYING APART; OR, WE
                                 JUST DON'T STICK TOGETHER LIKE WE USED TO.
                                 (NOT MARITAL PROBLEMS, WHICH ARE CODED
                                 BELOW).

                 222.  MARITAL PROBLEMS, E.G., ANY THAT ARE MENTIONED.

                 223.  TIME PRESSURE, E.G., WE'RE SO RUSHED, WE NEVER HAVE
                                      TIME FOR EACH OTHER OR THE THINGS WE
                                      WANT TO DO.

                 224.  CHILDREN, E.G., MY KIDS GIVE ME A LOT OF TROUBLE.
                                 (NOT EDUCATION, WHICH IS CODED BELOW);
                                 OTHER CHILD RELATED WORRIES.

                 225.  EDUCATION, E.G., MY KIDS ARE HAVING PROBLEMS AT
                                  SCHOOL; OR, GETTING A GOOD EDUCATION FOR
                                  MY CHILDREN.  (NOT REFERENCES TO THE
                                  SCHOOLS DOING A BAD JOB, WHICH ARE CODED
                                  AT THE COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL).

                 226.  FULFILLMENT, E.G., GETTING THE BEST FOR MY FAMILY; OR
                                    GETTING AHEAD, I WANT MORE FOR MY FAMILY;
                                    UNFULFILLED EXPECTATION ABOUT FAMILY
                                    MEMBERS.

                 229.  GENERAL COMMENTS ABOUT "FAMILY" QUALITY OF LIFE
                                        PROBLEMS NOT SPECIFICALLY CODED IN
                                        221-226.


                   COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL
                   ---------------------

                 230.  DISCRIMINATION, E.G., PEOPLE LIKE ME ARE GETTING
                                       KICKED AROUND; OR, NOBODY LOOKS OUT
                                       FOR THE LITTLE MAN ANYMORE; OR, ANY
                                       REFERENCE TO RACIAL OR ETHNIC
                                       DISCRIMINATION THAT THE RESPONDENT
                                       IS SUBJECT TO.

                 231.  DISCONTENT, E.G., THIS TOWN ISN'T ANY GOOD ANY MORE;
                                   OR, IT'S A MESS AROUND HERE; OR THIS
                                   COMMUNITY IS ROTTEN; OTHER MORALISTIC
                                   COMMENTS.

                 232.  XENOPHOBIA, E.G., THEY'RE GETTING IT WITHOUT WORKING
                                   FOR IT; OR, THE BLACKS; OR, THE
                                   WHITES; OR, RACIAL OR ETHNIC OR CLASS
                                   (E.G., THE POOR) REFERENCES WHEN IT
                                   IS CLEAR THAT RECONCILIATION IS NOT A
                                   GOAL; LABOR (OR THE UNIONS); MANAGEMENT
                                   (OR BOSSES).

                 233.  EDUCATION, E.G., THE SCHOOLS ARE NOT GOOD; OR, GOT TO
                                  IMPROVE THE SCHOOLS (NOT REFERENCES TO
                                  BUSING, WHICH WILL BE CODED BELOW); COST
                                  OF ELEMENTARY AND SECONDARY EDUCATION.

                 234.  COMMUNITY SERVICES, E.G., TRANSPORTATION INADEQUATE;
                                           GARBAGE AND SNOW REMOVAL; FIRE
                                           PROTECTION, ETC.

                 235.  BUSING AND SCHOOL INTEGRATION, NEGATIVE, E.G., ANY
                                         NEGATIVE REFERENCE TO RACE IN
                                         CONNECTION WITH SCHOOLS OR BUSING TO
                                         ACHIEVE INTEGRATION.

                 236.  BUSING AND SCHOOL INTEGRATION, POSITIVE, E.G., GOT
                                         TO MAKE SCHOOL INTEGRATION WORK.

                 237.  INSTITUTIONAL CONTACT, E.G., TROUBLE WITH POLICE
                                              OR WITH CITY OFFICIALS SUCH AS
                                              WELFARE WORKERS.  (NOT
                                              COMPLAINTS ABOUT THE LEVEL OF
                                              WELFARE SUPPORT, WHICH WILL BE
                                              CODED UNDER ECONOMIC/WEALTH).

                 238.  INTERGENERATIONAL HOSTILITY, E.G., YOUTH; OR, KIDS
                                                    HAVE NO RESPECT ANYMORE.

                 239.  INTERRACIAL HOSTILITY, E.G., THEY'RE TRYING TO MOVE
                                              TOO FAST; OR ANY NEGATIVE
                                              REFERENCE TO RACE NOT CONNECTED
                                              WITH EDUCATION.

                 240.  CITY POLLUTION AND ENVIRONMENT, E.G., THE AIR; THE
                                       WATER; THE NOISE (IN THIS CITY).

                 241.  GENERAL COMMENTS ABOUT "COMMUNITY/CLASS" QUALITY OF
                                       LIFE PROBLEMS NOT SPECIFICALLY CODED
                                       IN 230-340.


                   SOCIETY/NATIONAL-LEVEL
                   ----------------------

                 251.  NEGATIVE, E.G., THE DIRECTION THIS COUNTRY IS GOING IS
                                 ALL WRONG; OR, THE COUNTRY'S PRIORITES
                                 ARE BAD; OR, AMERICA IS A MESS.

                 252.  POSITIVE, E.G., AMERICA HAS TO LIVE UP TO ITS
                                 POTENTIAL OR THIS COUNTRY HAS TO BE
                                 STRAIGHTENED OUT; OR, GET AMERICA BACK
                                 ON THE TRACKS.

                 253.  POLLUTION AND ENVIRONMENT (IN GENERAL), E.G.,
                                       THE AIR; THE WATER; NOISE.

                 259.  COMMENTS ABOUT SPECIFIC "SOCIETY/NATIONAL"
                                       QUALITY OF LIFE PROBLEMS SUCH AS,
                                       CRIMINALS ARE TAKING OVER COUNTRY,
                                       ETC.


                   WORLD-LEVEL
                   -----------

                 261.  NEGATIVE, E.G., THE WORLD IS IN A MESS.

                 262.  POSITIVE, E.G., GOT TO WORK FOR MANKIND; OR, WORKING
                                 FOR PEACE.

                 269.  OTHER VAGUE REFERENCES TO "WORLD" QUALITY OF LIFE
                                   PROBLEMS.

                 ECONOMY/WEALTH--FINANCIAL PROBLEMS
                 ----------------------------------
                 ----------------------------------


                   SELF-LEVEL
                   ----------

                 311.  ANXIETY, GENERAL, E.G., I DON'T SEE HOW I CAN MAKE
                                       ENDS MEET; OR, I JUST DON'T HAVE
                                       ENOUGH MONEY; OR, I'M IN DEBT; LACK
                                       OF MONEY FOR THINGS I WANT/NEED.
                                       (NOTE: REFERENCES TO EGO, "I"
                                       CODED AT THE SELF-LEVEL;
                                       "WE" REFERENCES ARE CODED AT THE
                                       FAMILY-LEVEL; ANY MENTION OF WELFARE
                                       OR PUBLIC ASSISTANCE MOVES A RESPONSE
                                       TO THE COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL).

                 312.  ANXIETY, JOB, E.G., REFERENCES TO JOB SECURITY OR
                                     UNEMPLOYMENT; I'M WORRIED ABOUT MY JOB;
                                     OR, ABOUT LOSING MY JOB; OR, I'M
                                     OUT OF WORK; OR CAN'T FIND WORK.

                 313.  DISSATISFACTION, E.G., MY JOB DOESN'T PAY ENOUGH
                                        OR, I NEED MORE OVERTIME.  (NOT, THIS
                                        JOB IS BORING, SEE CODE 216).
                 314.  JOB REFERENCE - OTHER OR NA WHETHER 312, 313 OR 216
                                     (FOR NON-ECONOMIC JOB REFERENCES THAT
                                     AREN'T COVERED ELSEWHERE).

                 319.  OTHER GENERAL "SELF" LEVEL FINANCIAL PROBLEMS.

                 320.  DISCRIMINATION AGAINST WHITES; PREFERENTIAL TREATMENT
                                      GIVEN TO BLACKS.


                   FAMILY-LEVEL
                   ------------

                 321.  ANXIETY,  GENERAL, E.G., WE CAN'T MAKE ENDS MEET; OR
                                          WE JUST DON'T HAVE ENOUGH MONEY.
                                          (NOT, "COST OF LIVING," "INFLA-"
                                          TION," WHICH ARE TO BE CODED AT THE
                                          SOCIETY/NATIONAL-LEVEL).

                 322.  ANXIETY, JOB, E.G., MY HUSBAND'S (WIFE'S) EMPLOYMENT
                                     OR UNEMPLOYMENT; UNION OR FELLOW
                                     WORKERS MAKE JOB UNPLEASANT.

                 323.  DISSATISFACTION, E.G., MY HUSBAND'S (WIFE'S) JOB
                                        DOESN'T PAY ENOUGH.

                 329.  OTHER GENERAL "FAMILY" LEVEL FINANCIAL PROBLEMS.


                   COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL
                   ---------------------

                 331.  ANXIETY, E.G., WELFARE PAYMENTS INSUFFICIENT; OR,
                                CAN'T GET BY ON WELFARE; OR, CAN'T GET ENOUGH
                                FOOD STAMPS (NOTE:  SPECIFIC REFERENCES TO
                                SOCIAL SECURITY SHOULD BE CODED AT THE
                                SOCIETY/NATIONAL-LEVEL); OR, PEOPLE LIKE US
                                CAN'T MAKE IT.

                 332.  HOSTILITY, E.G., IT COSTS TOO MUCH TO LIVE AROUND
                                  HERE; (NOTE:  LOCALIZED REFERENCE PUTS THIS
                                  AT THE COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL); OR, TAXES
                                  HERE ARE TOO HIGH; OR THEY'RE GETTING ALL
                                  THE GOOD JOBS (REFERENCES TO RACIAL OR
                                  ETHNIC GROUPS).

                 339.  OTHER GENERAL "COMMUNITY/CLASS" LEVEL FINANCIAL
                                     PROBLEMS.


                   SOCIETY/NATIONAL-LEVEL
                   ----------------------

                 341.  ANXIETY, E.G., THIS RECESSION (DEPRESSION) IS WIPING
                                ME OUT; OR, I CAN'T MAKE IT ON SOCIAL
                                SECURITY.

                 342.  HOSTILITY, E.G., COST OF LIVING TOO HIGH; OR, TOO MUCH
                                  INFLATION; OR, TAXES TOO HIGH; OR, TOO MUCH
                                  GOVERNMENTAL WASTE.  (NOT HOWEVER COM-
                                  PLAINTS ABOUT FOREIGN AID, WHICH ARE CODED
                                  AT THE WORLD-LEVEL); COST OF COLLEGE.

                 349.  OTHER GENERAL "SOCIETY/NATIONAL" LEVEL FINANCIAL
                                     PROBLEMS.


                   WORLD-LEVEL


                 351.  ANXIETY, E.G., HOW CAN WE MAKE IT AGAINST FOREIGN
                                COMPETITION; OR, CHEAP FOREIGN LABOR.

                 352.  HOSTILITY, E.G., WE'RE GIVING AWAY ALL OUR WEALTH; OR,
                                  CHARITY BEGINS AT HOME; OR, NEGATIVE REF-
                                  ERENCES TO FOREIGN AID.

                 359.  OTHER GENERAL "WORLD" LEVEL FINANCIAL PROBLEMS.

                 HEALTH-MEDICAL PROBLEMS AND CONCERNS
                 ------------------------------------
                 ------------------------------------


                   SELF-LEVEL
                   ----------

                 411.  ANXIETY, E.G., ANY REFERENCE TO EGO'S MEDICAL OR
                                DENTAL PROBLEMS OR ILLNESS EXCEPT THOSE THAT
                                MENTION THE COSTS OF MEDICAL CARE; OR,
                                PERSONAL DRUG OR ALCOHOL PROBLEM, AGING.

                 412.  HOSTILITY, E.G. MY DOCTOR BILLS ARE TOO HIGH, (NOT
                                  REFERENCE TO MEDICARE, WHICH IS
                                  CODED AT THE SOCIETY/NATIONAL-LEVEL).

                 419.  OTHER GENERAL "SELF" LEVEL MEDICAL PROBLEMS.


                   FAMILY-LEVEL
                   ------------

                 421.  ANXIETY, E.G., ANY REFERENCE TO FAMILY MEDICAL OR
                                DENTAL PROBLEMS OR ILLNESS, EXCEPT THOSE
                                THAT MENTION THE COSTS OF MEDICAL CARE;
                                OR FAMILY DRUG OR ALCOHOL PROBLEM.


                 422.  HOSTILITY, E.G., MY FAMILY'S MEDICAL BILLS ARE TOO
                                  HIGH.

                 429.  OTHER GENERAL "FAMILY" LEVEL MEDICAL PROBLEMS.


                   COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL
                   ---------------------

                 432.  HOSTILITY, E.G., ANY NEGATIVE REFERENCE TO COMMUNITY
                                  HEALTH CARE OR HEALTH CARE FACILITIES;
                                  OR, HOSPITAL NO GOOD; DOCTORS DON'T CARE;
                                  TOO EXPENSIVE.

                 439.  OTHER GENERAL "COMMUNITY/CLASS" LEVEL MEDICAL
                                     PROBLEMS.


                   SOCIETY-NATIONAL LEVEL
                   ----------------------

                 442.  HOSTILITY, E.G., THE GOVERNMENT SHOULD DO SOMETHING
                                  ABOUT HEALTH CARE; OR, MEDICARE NOT
                                  ADEQUATE; OR, NEED NATIONAL HEALTH
                                  INSURANCE.


                   WORLD-LEVEL
                   -----------

                 549.  (NO CODES SEEM APPROPRIATE)

                 SAFETY/DANGER
                 -------------
                 -------------


                   SELF-LEVEL
                   ----------

                 611.  ANXIETY, E.G., I'M AFRAID TO GO OUT ON THE STREETS;
                                OR, I HAVE TO LIVE BEHIND LOCKED DOORS.

                 619.  OTHER GENERAL "SELF" LEVEL SAFETY/DANGER PROBLEMS.


                   FAMILY-LEVEL
                   ------------

                 621.  ANXIETY, E.G., MY FAMILY'S NOT SAFE AROUND HERE.

                 629.  OTHER GENERAL "FAMILY" LEVEL SAFETY/DANGER PROBLEMS.


                   COMMUNITY/CLASS-LEVEL
                   ---------------------

                 631.  ANXIETY, E.G., THERE'S SO MUCH CRIME, NO ONE IS SAFE
                                ANYMORE.  (STATEMENT WITHOUT REFERENCE TO
                                PERSONAL OR FAMILY DANGER).

                 632.  HOSTILITY, E.G., THEY'RE TOO LAX ABOUT DRUGS; OR, TOO
                                 MUCH PERMISSIVENESS; OR, THEY OUGHT TO
                                 DO SOMETHING ABOUT ALL THE CRIME.

                 639.  OTHER GENERAL "COMMUNITY/CLASS" LEVEL SAFETY/DANGER
                                     PROBLEMS.

                   ----------------------

                 642.  HOSTILITY, E.G., THE COURTS ARE TOO LENIENT ABOUT
                                  CRIME.

                 649.  OTHER GENERAL "SOCIETY/NATIONAL" LEVEL SAFETY/DANGER

                   -----------

                 651.  ANXIETY, E.G., EXPRESSIONS ABOUT FEAR OF WAR OR
                                COMMUNISM

                 659.  OTHER GENERAL "WORLD" LEVEL SAFETY/DANGER PROBLEMS.

                 998.  DK

>> 1974 PARTY/CANDIDATE MASTER CODE

                   THIS NOTE USED IN CONJUNCTION WITH QUESTIONS D2A, D2B, D3A,
                 D3B, D4A, D4B, D5A, D5B

                     ANY CATEGORY IN THE ENTIRE MASTER-CODE SET CAN BE A
                     REASON FOR LIKING OR DISLIKING SOMEONE, AND MOST CATE-
                     GORIES CAN REFER AS WELL TO PARTIES AND CANDIDATES.  FOR
                     EXAMPLE, "VERY LIBERAL IDEAS" CAN LOGICALLY BE A REASON
                     FOR LIKING OR DISLIKING EITHER PARTY OR EITHER CANDI-
                     DATE.  WHICH OF THE EIGHT LIKE/DISLIKE-PARTY/CANDIDATE
                     FIELDS A RESPONSE FALLS INTO CAN BE DETERMINED NORMALLY
                     BY THE QUESTION IT REFERS TO.

                     THE REASONS ARE BROKEN UP INTO SERIES OF HUNDREDS,
                     ACCORDING TO THE CONTENT AREA OF THE RESPONSE:

                          00 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY ONLY; PEOPLE
                             WITHIN PARTY
                          01 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY ONLY; PARTY
                             CHARACTERISTICS
                          02 HUNDRED SERIES---------------CANDIDATE ONLY; EX-
                             PERIENCE, ABILITY
                          03 HUNDRED SERIES---------------CANDIDATE ONLY;
                             LEADERSHIP QUALITIES
                          04 HUNDRED SERIES---------------CANDIDATE ONLY;
                             PERSONAL QUALITIES
                          05 HUNDRED SERIES---------------CANDIDATE ONLY;
                             PARTY CONNECTIONS
                          06 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             GOVERNMENT MANAGEMENT
                          07 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             MISCELLANEOUS
                          08 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             GOVT. ACTIVITY/PHILOSOPHY
                          09 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             DOMESTIC POLICIES
                          11 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             FOREIGN POLICIES
                          12 HUNDRED SERIES---------------PARTY OR CANDIDATE;
                             GROUP CONNECTIONS
                          99 HUNDRED SERIES---------------MISSING DATA CODES

                     THE PARTICULAR QUESTION RESPONDED TO DOES NOT ALWAYS
                     DETERMINE THE QUESTION UNDER WHICH THE PARTICULAR "MEN-
                     TION" IS TO BE CODED; E.G., AN ANTI-DEMOCRATIC RESPONSE
                     MAY BE FOUND IN Q.A8 (LIKES REPUBLICANS) OR Q.A12 (LIKES
                     NIXON).  A RESPONSE WAS NOT CODED AS REFERRING TO A
                     PARTY OR CANDIDATE UNLESS THE PARTY OR CANDIDATE WAS
                     ACTUALLY MENTIONED.  THAT IS, IF THE RESPONDENT LIKED
                     THE REPUBLICANS BECAUSE THEY ARE FIRM ON COMMUNISM, THIS
                     RESPONSE WAS NOT ALSO CODED AS A DISLIKE OF DEMOCRATS
                     BECAUSE THEY ARE SOFT ON COMMUNISM, UNLESS THIS WAS ALSO
                     CLEARLY STATED.  EVEN A COMPARATIVE REFERENCE SUCH AS
                     THE REPUBLICANS ARE FIRMER ON COMMUNISM THAN THE DEMO-
                     CRATS, WAS CODED ONLY AS A PRO-REPUBLICAN RESPONSE.  IF
                     R SAID "REPUBLICANS ARE FIRM ON COMMUNISM, DEMOCRATS ARE
                     NOT", BOTH RESPONSES WERE CODED.

>> 1974 FEELING THERMOMETER NOTE

                 THIS NOTE USED IN CONJUNCTION WITH QUESTIONS L1A THROUGH L2Z


                 THE FOLLOWING EXPLANATION OF THE FEELING THERMOMETER WAS
                 INCLUDED IN THE QUESTIONNAIRE AND READ BY THE INTERVIEWER
                 TO THE RESPONDENT:

                 I HAVE HERE A CARD ON WHICH THERE IS SOMETHING THAT LOOKS
                 LIKE A THERMOMETER.  WE CALL IT A FEELING THERMOMETER BE-
                 CAUSE IT MEASURES YOUR FEELINGS TOWARDS THESE PEOPLE.
                 HERE'S HOW IT WORKS.  IF YOU DON'T FEEL PARTICULARLY WARM OR
                 COLD TOWARD A PERSON, THEN YOU SHOULD PLACE HIM</HER> IN THE
                 MIDDLE OF THE THERMOMETER, AT THE FIFTY DEGREE MARK.  IF YOU
                 HAVE A WARM FEELING TOWARD A PERSON, OR FEEL FAVORABLY TO-
                 WARD HIM</HER>, YOU WOULD GIVE HIM</HER> A SCORE SOMEWHERE
                 BETWEEN FIFTY AND ONE HUNDRED DEGREES, DEPENDING ON HOW WARM
                 YOUR FEELING IS TOWARD THAT PERSON.  ON THE OTHER HAND, IF
                 YOU DON'T FEEL VERY FAVORABLY TOWARD A PERSON--THAT IS, IF
                 YOU DON'T CARE TOO MUCH FOR HIM</HER>--THEN YOU WOULD PLACE
                 HIM</HER> SOMEWHERE BETWEEN ZERO AND FIFTY DEGREES.  OF
                 COURSE IF YOU DON'T KNOW TOO MUCH ABOUT A PERSON JUST TELL ME
                 AND WE'LL GO ON TO THE NEXT NAME.

                 THE INTERVIEWER WAS INSTRUCTED TO TAKE SOME TIME TO EXPLAIN
                 HOW THE THERMOMETER WORKS, AND TO SHOW THE RESPONDENT HOW TO
                 LOCATE AN INDIVIDUAL WITH DEGREE LABELS.

                 IF THE RESPONDENT GAVE A RANGE, THE MIDPOINT WAS CODED
                 ROUNDING TO THE NEAREST ODD NUMBER IF NECESSARY.

                 ALL OTHER RESPONSES WERE CODED AS FOLLOWS:

                     00-96.  ACTUAL NUMBER OF "DEGREES"
                     50.  50 DEGREES; NO FEELING
                     97.  97-100 DEGREES

                     98.  DK (DOESN'T KNOW ANYTHING ABOUT "POSSIBLE
                          CANDIDATE")
                     99.  NA

                 THIS NOTE IS USED IN CONJUNCTION WITH QUESTION S42A(1) AND
                 S43A(1)

                 THE FIVE DIGIT CODING SCHEME USED IS FROM THE 1970 CENSUS OF
                 POPULATION AND HOUSING, 1970 FINAL REPORT: GEOGRAPHIC CODE
                 SCHEME.  WASHINGTON, D.C.: U.S. GOVERNMENT PRINTING OFFICE,
                 1972.

                 THE LAST THREE DIGITS ARE THE COUNTY.  THE COUNTY CODES ARE

                 WITHIN STATE.
